CORPORATE PROFILE                                                   公司簡介
SHOUGANG FUSHAN RESOURCES GROUP LIMITED                             首鋼福山資源集團有限公司
is one of the most sizable integrated coking coal corporations in   為中國中西部 最具規模之綜合焦煤企業之一。
CENTRAL-WESTERN CHINA.
Taking Shanxi Province as its major investment base, it             以山西省作為主要投資基地，
is principally engaged in MINING OF COKING COAL,                    主要從事 焦煤開採、原焦煤及精焦煤生產及銷
PRODUCTION AND SALES OF RAW AND CLEAN COKING
                                                                    售業務。
COAL.
The Group has three premium operating coking coal mines and         本集團現有三座在產的優質焦煤礦及三座洗煤廠。
three coal preparation plants.

OPERATING MINES                                                        營運中之煤礦

XINGWU COAL MINE                                                       興無煤礦

•   6 km south of Liulin County and the mining right area extended     •   位於柳林縣以南6公里，採礦權區佔地約
    over 11.6 sq. km and spans 4.5 km east to west and 4.5 km              11.6平方公里，東西相距4.5公里，南北相距
    north to south                                                         4.5公里

•   Operation commenced in 1968                                        •   於一九六八年開始營運

•   Annual approved raw coking coal production capacity: 1.75          •   核准年原焦煤生產能力：175萬噸
    million tonnes

•   Operate a coal preparation plant with annual designed input        •   經營一座年入洗量為120萬噸之洗煤廠（於二
    processing capacity of 1.2 million tonnes (put into operation in       零零二年十月投產）
    October 2002)

•   Mainly production of hard coking coal                              •   主要生產硬焦煤

OPERATING MINES (continued)                                               營運中之煤礦（續）

COAL CHARACTERISTICS                                                      煤炭特徵

•     Located within the Lishi-Liulin mining area of Hedong coalfield,    •   位於河東煤田之離柳礦區，該區為中國優質
      one of China’s key reserve areas for high-quality hard coking           硬焦煤之主要儲量區域之一。
      coal.

•     Regarded as “panda coal” because of its scarcity and high           •   由於其稀有性和高經濟價值，被譽為「熊貓
      economic value.                                                         煤」。

•     The Group’s coking coal is of particularly good quality due to      •   本集團之焦煤發熱值高及焦結度強，加上灰
      its high calorific value and caking index with low ash and sulfur       份及硫份低，品質優越，使之非常適合用於
      content characteristics that are highly desirable for coke and          生產焦炭及鋼鐵。
      steel making.

     3.2 Risk Management                                                   3.2 風險管理

        The Group continues to strengthen the development of the                本集團持續加強風險管理體系和內控系
        risk management system and internal control system, carry               統的建設，開展全面風險防控工作，提
        out comprehensive risk prevention and control, improve                  升公司抵禦風險的能力，優化審計監察
        the company’s anti-risk ability, optimise the reform of audit           組織的變革，確保本集團生產運營合規
        and supervision agency, ensure the compliance of the                    合法，防範經營風險和法律合規風險。
        Group’s production and operation, and prevent business
        risks and legal compliance risks.

        The structure of the Group’s risk management system                     本集團的風險管理系統建基於：1）風險
        based on: 1) Risk Governance Structure; and 2) Risk                     管治架構；及2）風險管理程序。
        Management Procedures.

        Risk Governance Structure                                               風險管治架構

        The Group’s risk governance structure is based on a                     本集團以三道防線模式為基礎來支援董
        “Three Lines of Defense” model, with oversight by the                   事會對風險管理系統的持續監督，並透
        Board on an ongoing basis and is reviewed by the Audit                  過審核委員會審議的檢討結果向董事會
        Committee and subsequently reported to the Board on the                 匯報風險管理系統是否有效。
        effectiveness of the risk management system.

   3.2 Risk Management (continued)                                     3.2 風險管理（續）

      Under the “Three Lines of Defense” model, major risk                  在三道防線模式下，各防線的主要風險
      management functions and measures in each line are as                 管理職能和措施如下：
      follows:

       1st Line of Defense:                 2nd Line of Defense:                  3rd Line of Defense:
       Risk Management Function             Risk Monitoring Function              Independent Assurance Function
       第一道防線：風險管理職能                         第二道防線：風險監察職能                          第三道防線：獨立驗證職能

       1) Identify and evaluate risks,    1)     Monitor design appropriateness   1)   Independent assessment
          adopt measures to manage risks         and implementation of risk            on the appropriateness
          識別及評估風險、採取措施以                          management procedures                 and effectiveness of risk
          管理風險                                   監督風險管理程序的設計合適                         management system
       2) Self-assessment on the                 性和執行有效性                               對風險管理系統的合適性和有
          effectiveness of the measures,  2)     Promote delivery of risk              效性進行獨立評價
          adjust timely to mitigate risks        management information
          自我檢查措施的成效，適時調                          促進風險管理資訊的傳達
          整方法以減輕風險


      Risk Management Procedures                                            風險管理程序

      The Group adopted and implemented the Group's risk                    本集團參照全國反虛假財務報告委員會
      management policy and procedures that is appropriate                  下屬的發起人委員會刊發的企業風險管
      to the Group by making reference to the Committee of                  理框架建立和推行適合本集團的風險管
      Sponsoring Organisation of the Treadway Commission                    理政策和程序。
      Enterprise Risk Management Framework.

      The Board is responsible for assessing and determining the            董事會負責評估和釐定本集團達成策略
      Group’s risk appetite regarding the nature and magnitude              目標時所接納的風險性質及程度，並建
      and constructs a risk management procedures allowing a                立了一個自上而下，由下而上的風險管
      tone at the top and bottom up reporting.                              理程序。

        Based on the principles of “those who take charge of                  本 集 團 以「管 生 產 經 營 必 須 管 安 全」，
        production and operation must also emphasise safety” and             「誰主管、誰負責」的原則，全面落實各

      Three Preventions”

      (“one ventilation and three preventions”) for safe               （以下簡稱「一通三防」  ）工作，建立以總

      and three preventions” management system led by the               立一通三防部監管、指導礦對一通三防
      chief engineer. The Group establishes one ventilation and         方面的盡職履職情況。部門負責編製
      three prevention department to supervise and guide the           「一通三防」管理實施方案，制定並檢查
      due diligence on “one ventilation and three prevention” in        通風、瓦斯抽採等工作目標，組織月度
      the mine. The responsible department prepares the guide           例會，並對三座在產煤礦開展季度一通
      on “one ventilation and three preventions” management,            三防專項檢查。於二零二一年度，三礦
      formulates and inspects the objectives of ventilation and         共查出隱患1,336條，已全部整改。

     6.1 Coping with Climate Risks                                           6.1 應對氣候風險

          Climate change is a systemic challenge facing the world.                氣候變化是全球面對的系統性挑戰。本

          and increase the share of renewable energy, so as to not                排放，支持《聯合國氣候變化框架公約》
          only effectively reduce greenhouse gas emissions, but also              締約方所達成的《巴黎協定》2ºC控溫目

          stipulated in Paris Agreement which reached by the parties

      Environmental Management Organisational System                       環境管理機構體系

      The Group implements the environmental protection                    本集團認真貫徹執行國家和上級環境保
      guidelines, policies, laws, regulations, standards and               護的方針、政策、法律、法規、標準和
      environmental protection management policies of the                  各項環境保護管理制度，三座在產煤礦
      state and the supervising units. The three coal mines in             分別設立環境保護委員會，主任由礦長
      production set up environmental protection committees                擔任，副主任為技術礦長，委員由各礦
      respectively. The mine manager acts as the chairman and              有關部門的主要負責人組成，辦公室設
      the technical manager acts as the deputy chairman, and the           在礦環保科，負責環保日常監督管理工
      committee is composed of the main principals of various              作，辦公室主任由環保科長擔任。於二
      departments in the mine. The committee is supported by               零二一年度，本集團有一座在產煤礦環
      an office which is set in the mine environmental protection          境管理體系認證（ISO 14001）處於有效
      division and it is responsible for the regular supervision and       期內，其餘兩座在產煤礦正在準備相關
      management of environmental protection. The chief of                 體系的認證工作中。
      the environmental protection division acts as the principal
      of the office. In 2021, one coal mine in production of the
      group had an effective environmental management system
      certificated by ISO 14001, and the other two coal mines in
      production are preparing the certification.

      Supervision, Management and Assessment System                        監督管理考核制度

      Environmental protection departments are set up under the            本集團三座在產煤礦下設環保科，負責
      three coal mines in the Group. The department supervises             水污染防治、大氣污染防治、固體廢
      and manages water pollution prevention, air pollution                物處置、污染物達標排放控制的監督管
      prevention, solid waste disposal and pollutant emission              理，涵蓋各礦涉及環保的區域，重點為
      control in the areas under the environmental responsibility          主井煤場、生活污水處理站、礦井水處
      of each mine, mainly on the main coal yards, domestic                理廠、第三方車隊、第三方運營公司
      sewage treatment plants, mine water treatment plants,                等。環保科對全礦涉及環保的部門採用
      third-party fleets, company operated by third parties,               監督檢查與積分考核相結合，根據現
      etc. The environmental protection department combines                場發現問題，填寫《環保問題監督檢查
      supervision and inspection with the grade assessment of              表》，以書面形式下發到相關部門，要
      the departments involved in environmental protection                 求各部門限期整改並驗收銷號，對要求
      in the whole mine. What’s more, the Group will fill in               期限內未整改或情節嚴重的部門責任
      the Form of Environmental Problems Supervision and                   人、當事人給予扣除積分考核。
      Inspection for any problems found on site, and sends it to
      relevant departments in written form, requiring relevant
      departments to rectify within a time limit and canceling
      the name list after being accepted. The principal of the
      department and the party concerned who fails to rectify
      within the time limit or have other worse behaviors will be
      deducted for integral assessment.

         Case: 3MW Low-Concentration Gas Power Generation                   案例：寨崖底煤礦3MW低濃度瓦斯發電項
         Project in Zhaiyadi Coal Mine                                      目

         The Group has been building JunLiu Low-Concentration               本集團於二零二零年起建設君柳低濃度瓦
         Gas Power Station Project since 2020 to safely deliver the         斯發電站項目，將寨崖底煤礦生產過程中
         low concentration gas extracted and produced by Zhaiyadi           抽採的低濃度瓦斯，通過瓦斯管道及安全
         Coal Mine to the low concentration gas heat island through         輸送設備安全的送至低濃度瓦斯熱能島
         gas pipeline and safety transmission equipment. Through a          中。低濃度瓦斯在熱能島中通過一系列的
         series of safety techniques and patented technologies, low         安全措施和專利技術，進行安全穩定的燃
         concentration gas can burn safely and stably on the heat           燒，瓦斯燃燒產生的高溫煙氣直接進入餘
         island. The high-temperature flue gas generated by gas             熱鍋爐，高溫煙氣在餘熱鍋爐中依次經過
         combustion was transmitted directly into the waste heat boiler.    蒸汽過熱器、蒸發器、省煤器和節能器，
         The high-temperature flue gas successively passes through the      最後溫度降低到約80℃後經過引風機和煙
         steam superheater, evaporator, economiser and energy saver in      氣管道進入煙囪排空。
         the waste heat boiler. At last, after the temperature is reduced
         to about 80℃, the flue gas enters the chimney through an
         induced draft fan and flue gas pipeline to emit.

         The waste heat boiler absorbs the heat energy of high-             餘熱鍋爐吸收高溫煙氣的熱能將除鹽水加
         temperature flue gas and heats the demineralised water to          熱產生P = 2.5MPa/T = 400℃的過熱蒸汽，
         produce superheated steam (whose P = 2.5MPa/T = 400℃).             過熱蒸汽經過蒸汽管網送至汽輪發電機
         The superheated steam is sent to the steam turbine generator       組，蒸汽在汽輪機中進行全凝發電，最後
         unit through the steam pipe network; the steam is fully            低壓蒸汽經過凝汽器冷凝成水，經過凝結
         condensed in the steam turbine for power generation; the           水泵加壓送至除氧器作為鍋爐用水的主要
         low-pressure steam is condensed into the water through the         部分，形成循環，餘熱鍋爐排污等造成的
         condenser and pressurised by the condensate pump to the            損失水量由除鹽水系統經過汽輪機的凝汽
         deaerator as the main part of boiler water to form a cycle,        器補充。汽輪發電機組發電電壓10KV，直
         The water loss caused by waste heat boiler blowdown is             接併入煤礦的10KV變電站，供煤礦生產自
         supplemented by the demineralised water system through             用，減少外購電力消耗量。
         the condenser of the steam turbine. The generating voltage
         of the steam turbine generator unit is 10kV, which is directly
         connected to the 10kV substation of the coal mine for the
         production and self-use of the coal mine, so as to reduce the
         purchased power consumption.

        equipment on the mime and underground. Specifically, the         高耗能設備，將原有皮帶運輸機驅動部分
        Group changed the driver of the original belt conveyor to        改為永磁電動滾筒驅動。相比原有驅動系
        a permanent magnet electric drum drive. Compared with            統，永磁滾筒系統傳動效率提升達25%，
        the original drive system, the transmission efficiency of the    用電能耗降低30%，並有效減少設備維護

        and the power consumption was reduced by 30%. Also, the          油消耗。

        Prevention Measures

        In order to prevent the pandemic, the Group has formulated        本集團為做好防範疫情工作，制定了較為
        comprehensive “five haves and one grid” measures:                 完善的「五有一網格」的防控舉措：

        “Five haves” means that have pandemic prevention guidelines,      「五有」即：有疫情防控指南，有防控管理
        have prevention management policy and responsible party,          制度和責任人，有適量防護物資儲備，有
        have an appropriate amount of protective materials backups,       屬地醫療衛生力量指導支持，有隔離場所
        have guidance and support from local medical and health           和轉運安排準備等措施。
        forces, have places for isolation and transfer arrangements and
        preparations.

        “One Grid” means that information registration, investigation     「一網格」即：做好假期返公司員工、外來
        and health monitoring for employees, outsiders, persons from      人員、來自中高風險地區人員、入境人員
        medium and high-risk areas, inbound personnel returning           等重點人群的信息登記、摸排和日常健康
        from holiday, supervise and urge the implementation of            監測工作，督促落實好個人防護措施，強
        personal protection measures, and emphasise self-isolation and    調出現發熱等症狀後的自我隔離和報告。
        reporting when fever and other symptoms appears. Increasing       加強巡迴督查，發現異常情況及時了解、
        patrol inspection and timely understanding, verifying and         核實和報告。
        reporting of abnormal conditions.

        prices have been rising. To adapt to the market environment       團為適應市場環境，供給煤炭資源，不斷
        and supply coal resources, and expand production and              拓展生產經營，通過福利煤補償價，幫助

      Case: Preferential Compensation Price of Coal (continued)          案例：福利煤補償價格（續）

      The Group carried out the following four measures to provide       本集團開展以下四個措施，為村鎮提供煤
      coal welfare for villages and towns and quickly determine the      炭福利，快速研究定奪福利煤補償金額。
      amount of preferential compensation price of coal.

      1.   Conduct market research on preferential coal prices;          1.   針對煤炭福利價格進行市場調研；

      2.   Visit and align with preferential coal prices in the region   2.   針對本集團所在區域、兄弟企業等有
           where the Group is located and peer enterprises;                   關煤炭福利價格等進行走訪與對標；

      3.   Organise a discussion for residents in villages and towns     3.   組織村鎮中對煤炭福利價格持有保留
           who has opinions about preferential coal prices;                   態度的居民進行座談交流會；

      4.   After investigation, benchmarking, visit and                  4.   在調研、對標、走訪、與居民交流
           communication with residents which based on the data               後，依據了解的煤炭福利相關數據與
           and materials on coal welfare, senior management hold              資料後，領導幹部舉行總經理辦公會
           a general manager meeting to study and agree on the                研究議定煤炭福利補償價格，為村鎮
           preferential compensation price of coal, so as to provide          等區域提供福利煤。
           welfare coal for villages and towns and other areas.

   7.4 Community Building (continued)                                      7.4 社區共創（續）

        Case: Protection of Villagers’ Rights And Interests                案例：村民權益保障

        In 2021, the Group developed a set of operating procedures         於二零二一年，本集團從保障村民權益為
        for relocation from the perspective of protecting the rights       出發點，針對搬遷制定了一套相應的操作
        and interests of villagers. First, the coal mine reports to the    流程。首先以煤礦書面上報管理中心，議
        management center in writing to discuss a feasible operation       定具有可行性的操作方案，並聯合鄉村形
        plan. Then the Group forms a relocation proposal together          成搬遷議項，雙方共同協商議定成具體的
        with the villages. The two sides negotiate and agree on a          搬遷方案。本集團在方案實施的過程中，

CHAIRMAN AND MANAGING DIRECTOR                                                  主席及董事總經理

The roles of chairman and managing director are separate and                    為加強主席與董事總經理的獨立性及問責性，彼
exercised by different individuals to reinforce their independence and          等的角色已予區分，並由不同人士擔任。丁汝才
accountability. Mr. Ding Rucai is the Chairman and Mr. Fan Wenli                先生為主席，而范文利先生則為本公司的董事總
is the managing director of the Company. The Chairman provides                  經理。主席的職責負責領導董事會，確保董事會
leadership for the Board and ensures that the Board works effectively           有效地運作，且履行其職責；本公司董事總經理
and performs its responsibilities. The managing director of the                 則肩負行政總裁整體的職務，負責本集團整體的
Company has overall chief executive responsibility for the Group’s              業務發展及日常管理。主席與本公司董事總經理
business development and day-to-day management generally. The                   之間職責的分工已予清楚界定並以書面列載。
division of responsibilities between the Chairman and the managing
director of the Company is clearly established and set out in writing.

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                            股息政策

The Board formalised and adopted a dividend policy for the Company         董事會為本公司正式制訂及採納於二零一八年十
which took effect from 12 December 2018 and it aims to allow the           二月十二日起生效之股息政策，旨在讓股東分享
shareholders to participate in the Company’s profits whilst retaining      本公司溢利時，同時預留足夠儲備以供本集團日
adequate reserves for the future growth of the Group.                      後發展之用。

The Company intends to distribute not less than 40% of its net profit      本公司擬將每個財政年度不少於40%的股東應佔
attributable to the shareholders (not less than dividend payout ratio      純利（股息發放率不少於40%)分派為股息，惟董
of 40%) in each financial year as dividends. However, the Board will       事會於考慮宣派或建議派發股息時，將會考慮本
take into account certain factors of the Group when considering            集團多項因素，包括實際及預期財務表現、保留
the declaration or recommendation of dividends, which includes the         溢利及可供分派儲備、流動資金狀況、整體經濟
actual and expected financial performance, retained earnings and           狀況、本集團業務的營運週期、可能對本集團業
distributable reserves, liquidity position, general economic conditions,   務或財務表現及狀況造成影響的其他內外因素以
business cycle of the Group’s businesses and other internal or external    及董事會可能認為相關的其他因素。
factors that may have an impact on the businesses or financial
performance and position of the Group, and other factors that the
Board may consider relevant.

BASIS FOR OPINION                                                          意見基礎

We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on            我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審計準則

         accounting

          (i)   Subsidiaries                                                   (i)   附屬公司

                Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                  附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權之所
                entities) over which the Group has control. The Group                有實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集
                controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or                  團從參與實體業務而承擔取得其可
                has rights to, variable returns from its involvement                 變回報之風險或享有可變回報之權
                with the entity and has the ability to affect those                  利，並有能力透過其對實體業務之
                returns through its power to direct the activities of                主導權影響該等回報時，則本集團
                the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the             控制該實體。附屬公司於控制權轉
                date on which control is transferred to the Group.                   移至本集團當日起全面綜合入賬，
                They are deconsolidated from the date that control                   並於終止控制權當日起終止綜合入
                ceases.                                                              賬。

                The acquisition method of accounting is used to                      本集團採用收購會計法將業務合併
                account for business combinations by the Group (refer                入賬（見附註2.3）。
                to note 2.3).

                Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealised                   集團內公司間交易、結餘及集團內
                gains on transactions between group companies are                    公司間交易未變現收益均會對銷。
                eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless             未變現虧損亦會對銷，惟該交易有
                the transaction provides evidence of an impairment                   證據顯示已轉讓資產出現減值則除
                of the transferred asset. Accounting policies of                     外。附屬公司之會計政策已在有需
                subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to                    要時作出更改，以確保與本集團所
                ensure consistency with the policies adopted by the                  採納有關政策一致。
                Group.

                Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of               附屬公司業績及權益中之非控股權
                subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated                益分別於綜合損益及其他全面收益

          (ii) Associates                                                         (ii) 聯營公司

               recognised at cost.

               Under the equity method of accounting, the                             根據權益會計法，投資初步以成本

               adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of                  認本集團應佔被投資方之收購後溢
               the post-acquisition profits or losses of the investee                 利或虧損，及於其他全面收益內確
               in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of movements                  認本集團應佔被投資方之其他全面
               in other comprehensive income of the investee in                       收益變動。聯營公司及合營企業之
               other comprehensive income. Dividends received                         已收或應收股息被確認為投資賬面
               or receivable from associates and joint ventures are                   值之扣減。
               recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of
               the investment.

               accounted investment equals or exceeds its interest in                 法計算之投資之虧損等同或超逾其

               receivables, the Group does not recognise further                      抵押長期應收款項，則本集團並不
               losses, unless it has incurred obligations or made                     確認進一步之虧損，除非其代表其
               payments on behalf of the other entity.                                他實體負有責任或支付款項，則另
                                                                                      作別論。

     2.14 Inventories                                                         2.14 存貨

          Inventories are initially recognised at cost, and subsequently         存貨初步根據成本確認，並於其後按成
          at the lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost is                 本及可變現淨值兩者之較低者確認。
          calculated using weighted average method, and in the                   成本按加權平均法基準計算，倘為製成
          case of finished goods, comprises direct materials, direct             品，則包括直接物料、直接人工及適當
          labour and an appropriate proportion of overheads. Net                 比例之間接費用。可變現淨值為於日常
          realisable value represented the estimated net selling prices          業務過程中之估計銷售淨價格減作出銷
          in the ordinary course of business less the estimated costs            售所需之估計成本。
          necessary to make the sale.


     2.15 Trade receivables                                                   2.15 應收貿易賬項

          Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for                   應收貿易賬項乃就日常業務過程中因出
          goods sold or services performed in the ordinary course of             售貨品或提供服務而應收客戶款項。應
          business. They are generally due for settlement within 60              收貿易賬項一般60至90天內到期結付，
          to 90 days and therefore are all classified as current.                因此全部分類為流動。

          Trade receivables are recognised initially at the amount               應收貿易賬項初步按屬無條件之代價金
          of consideration that is unconditional unless they                     額確認，除非按公平值確認時含有重大
          contain significant financing components, when they                    財務元素則作別論。本集團持有應收貿
          are recognised at fair value. The Group holds the trade                易賬項，旨在收取合約現金流量，因此
          receivables with the objective to collect the contractual              其後採用實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。
          cash flows and therefore measures them subsequently                    有關本集團之應收貿易賬項會計處理方

     2.20 Current and deferred income tax                                     2.20 即期及遞延所得稅

          The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax            期內所得稅開支或抵免為基於各司法權
          payable on the current period’s taxable income based on               區之適用所得稅率計算之即期應課稅收
          the applicable income tax rate for each jurisdiction adjusted         入（由暫時差額及未動用稅項虧損導致
          by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable        之遞延稅項資產及負債變動調整）之應
          to temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                    付稅項。

          and its subsidiaries and associates operate and generate              之稅務法例計算。管理層就適用稅務法
          taxable income. Management periodically evaluates                     例詮釋所規限之情況定期評估報稅表之
          positions taken in tax returns with respect to situations in          狀況，並考慮稅務機關是否有可能接受
          which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation          不確定的稅收待遇。本集團根據最可能
          and considers whether it is probable that a taxation                  的金額或預期值來衡量其稅收餘額，具
          authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment. The                 體取決於哪種方法可以更好地預測不確
          Group measures its tax balances either based on the most              定性的解決方法。
          likely amount or the expected value, depending on which
          method provides a better prediction of the resolution of
          the uncertainty.

          Deferred income tax                                                   遞延所得稅

          Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the liability           遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產及負債之
          method, on temporary differences arising between the tax               稅基與其於綜合財務報表內之賬面值
          bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts in          兩者之暫時差額悉數作出撥備。然而，
          the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred               倘遞延所得稅負債於商譽初步獲確認時
          income tax liabilities are not recognised if they arise from           產生，則遞延所得稅負債不予確認。倘
          the initial recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is            遞延所得稅乃因於業務合併以外交易中
          also not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of        初步確認資產或負債而產生，而於交易
          an asset or a liability in a transaction other than a business         時不會影響會計處理及應課稅損益，則
          combination that at the time of the transaction affects                遞延所得稅亦不予入賬。遞延所得稅採
          neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred                用在報告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈之稅率
          income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that              （及稅法）及於有關遞延所得稅資產變現
          have been enacted or substantially enacted by the end of               或遞延所得稅負債結算時預期將會應用
          the reporting period and are expected to apply when the                之稅率（及稅法）釐定。
          related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred

     2.23 Provisions                                                            2.23 撥備

          Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                   當本集團因過往事件須承擔現有法律或
          legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events,             推定責任，而解除責任將很可能需要資
          it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required             源流出，且有關金額已可作可靠估算時
          to settle the obligation and the amount can be reliably                  會確認撥備，惟不會就日後之經營虧損
          estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future                      確認任何撥備。
          operating losses.

          Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                     倘出現多項類似責任，是否需要資源流
          likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement                出以解除責任乃經考慮整體責任類別
          is determined by considering the class of obligations as a               後釐定。即使就同類責任中任何一項出
          whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an            現資源流出之可能性不大，仍會確認撥
          outflow with respect to any one item included in the same                備。
          class of obligations may be small.

     2.25 Interest income                                                   2.25 利息收入

          Interest income on financial assets measured at amortised           來自按攤銷成本計量之財務資產以及按
          cost and financial assets measured at fair value through            公平值計入其他全面收益之財務資產之
          other comprehensive income calculated using the effective           利息收入乃按實際利率法計算，並於綜
          interest method is recognised in the consolidated statement         合損益表確認。
          of profit or loss.

          Interest income is presented as finance income where                利息收入乃呈列為財務收入，而其乃自
          it is earned from financial assets that are held for cash           持有作現金管理目的之財務資產賺取。
          management purposes.

          Interest income is calculated by applying the effective             利息收入乃就財務資產之賬面總值採用
          interest rate to the gross carrying amount of a financial           實際利率計算得出，惟其後出現信貸減
          asset except for financial assets that subsequently become          值之財務資產除外。就信貸減值之財務
          credit-impaired. For credit-impaired financial assets,              資產而言，實際利率乃應用於財務資產
          the effective interest rate is applied to the net carrying          之賬面淨值（扣除虧損撥備後）。
          amount of the financial asset (after deduction of the loss
          allowance).

          income. However, the investment may need to be tested

     2.28 Leases                                                            2.28 租賃

          Prepaid lease payments are classified as land use rights,            預付租賃款項分類為土地使用權，其他
          other leases are recognised as right-of-use assets and a             租賃則於本集團可用該租賃資產當日確
          corresponding liability at the date at which the leased              認為使用權資產及相應負債。租賃條款
          asset is available for use by the Group. Lease terms are             按個別基準磋商，並包含各項不同條款
          negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range           及條件。除了出租人所持有租賃資產之
          of different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do           抵押權益外，租賃協議概不施加任何契
          not impose any covenants other than the security interests           諾。租賃資產不得用作借款抵押。
          in the leased assets that are held by the lessor. Leased
          assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.

          Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially            一項租賃產生之資產及負債初步按現值
          measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include         基準計量。租賃負債計及固定款項之現
          the net present value of the fixed payments (including               值淨額（包括實質固定款項）減任何應收
          in-substance fixed payments), less any lease incentives              租賃優惠。
          receivable.

          The lease payments are discounted using the interest                 租賃款項使用租賃隱含之利率貼現。倘
          rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily           無法輕易釐定該利率（本集團的租賃一
          determined, which is generally the case for leases in the            般屬此類情況），則使用承租人之增量
          group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,              借款利率，即個別承租人在類似經濟環
          being the rate that the individual lessee would have to              境中按類似條款及條件借入獲得與使用
          pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of              權資產價值類似的資產所需資金必須支
          similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic        付的利率。
          environment with similar terms and conditions.

     3.1 Financial risk factors                                                    3.1 財務風險因素

          exposure to market risk, including changes in interest rates                  匯率變動。一般而言，本集團採取保守
          and currency exchange rates. Generally, the Group employs                     之風險管理策略。由於董事認為本集團
          a conservative strategy regarding its risk management. As                     面對之市場風險維持於低水平，故本集

          risk is kept at a minimum level, the Group has not used                       進行對沖。本集團面對最重大之財務風

          (c)   Liquidity risk                                                       (c)   流動資金風險

                Liquidity risk relates to the risk that the Group will                     流動資金風險與本集團無法履行其

                risk in respect of settlement of trade payables and                        資責任以及其現金流量管理有關之
                its financing obligations, and also in respect of its                      流動資金風險。本集團之目標為將
                cash flow management. The Group’s objective is                             流動資產及資金承擔維持於合適水
                to maintain an appropriate level of liquid assets                          平，以應付其短期及長期流動資金
                and committed lines of funding to meet its liquidity                       需求。
                requirements in the short and long term.

                The Group manages its liquidity needs by carefully                         本集團於日常業務中謹慎監察潛在
                monitoring expected payments for potential                                 投資及現金流出之預期付款，以管

                lookout periods are identified on a monthly basis.

          and the risks specific to the liability, such as change of the         項義務的修訂將以適當的貼現率予以確
          borrowing rate and inflation rate in the market. As changes            認。
          in estimates occur (such as mine plan revisions, changes in
          estimated costs, or changes in timing of the performance
          of reclamation activities), the revisions to the obligation will
          be recognised at the appropriate discount rate.

     (iii) The Group’s leasing activities and how these                      (iii) 本集團之租賃活動及如何將其
           are accounted for                                                       入賬

          The Group leases various lands and offices premises. Rental           本集團租用多項土地及辦公場地。租用
          contracts are typically made for fixed periods of 1 to 34             合約通常以固定期限1至34年不等。
          years.

          Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                 租賃條款按個別基準磋商，並包含各項
          contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.               不同條款及條件。除出租人所持有租
          The lease agreements do not impose any covenants other                賃資產之抵押權益外，租賃協議概不施
          than the security interests in the leased assets that are held        加任何契諾。租賃資產不得用作借款抵
          by the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as security for          押。
          borrowing purposes.

34. RESERVES                                                                34. 儲備

     (a) Statutory reserve                                                       (a) 法定儲備

          In accordance with the relevant laws and regulations of                    根據中國相關法律及法規，中國附屬公
          the PRC, the PRC subsidiaries are required to appropriate                  司須按照中國會計規例編製提撥10%
          10% of its profit after tax, prepared in accordance with the               之除稅後溢利至法定儲備基金，直至法
          accounting regulation in the PRC, to the statutory reserve                 定儲備結餘達註冊資本之50%為止。該
          fund until the statutory reserve balance reaches 50% of                    儲備可用作扣減任何虧損或撥充實繳資
          the registered capital. Such reserve may be used to reduce                 本。
          any losses incurred or for capitalisation as paid-up capital.


     (b) Other reserves                                                          (b) 其他儲備

          Pursuant to the relevant PRC regulations, the Group is                     根據相關中國法規，本集團須按照產量
          required to transfer production maintenance fee, safety                    等相關基準按固定利率將維簡費、安全
          fund and other expense of similar nature (“production                      費及類似性質的其他開支（
                                                                                                「維簡基金」）
          and maintenance funds”) at fixed rates based on relevant                   轉撥至專項儲備賬。維簡基金可於維簡
          bases, such as production volume, to a specific reserve                    及安全措施產生開支或資本開支時動
          account. The production and maintenance funds could                        用。已動用的維簡基金金額將從專項儲
          be utilised when expenses or capital expenditures on                       備賬轉撥至保留盈利。
          production maintenance and safety measures are incurred.
          The amount of production and maintenance funds utilised
          would be transferred from the specific reserve account to
          retained earnings.
